      description: ''
      parameters: []

        required: true

                  type: required
                  description: 'The end date of the report.'
                  example: '2021-02-02'
                  nullable: false
                statement_types:
                  type: array
                  description: 'An array of statement types to generate. Allowed types: ACCOUNT_STATEMENT, PAYMENTS, REFUNDS, WITHDRAWALS.'
                  example:
                    - ACCOUNT_STATEMENT
                    - PAYMENTS
                  items:
                    type: string
                isStamped:
                  type: boolean
                  description: ''
                  example: false
                  nullable: false
              required:
                - start_date
                - statement_types

                  type: string
                  description: 'Must be a valid email address.'

                  nullable: false
                country:
                  type: string
                  description: 'Must be in the ISO 4217 standard.'
                  example: US
                  nullable: false
                  enum:
                    - US
                    - NG
                    - AE
                    - etc
                nationality:
                  type: string
                  description: 'ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 nationality code.'
                  example: NG
                  nullable: false
                phone:
                  type: string
                  description: ''
                  example: et
                  nullable: false
                freelance_category_id:
                  type: integer
                  description: 'The <code>id</code> of an existing record in the freelance_categories table.'
                  example: 13
                  nullable: false
                password:
                  type: string
                  description: 'The password must be at least 8 characters, must contain at least one special character and one number'
                  example: 'm"_InO:|'
                  nullable: false
              required:
                - firstname
                - lastname
                - email
                - country
                - nationality
                - phone

  /api/v1/refresh-token:
    post:
      summary: 'Refresh an access token.'
      operationId: refreshAnAccessToken
      description: 'Exchanges a one-time refresh token for a new access and refresh token pair.'
      parameters: []
      responses:
        200:
          description: Success
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                example:
                  msg: 'Token refreshed successfully.'
                  data:
                    access_token: eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...
                    refresh_token: eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...
                    expires_in: 3600
                    expiry_date: '2026-08-11 15:30:00'
                  success: true
                  code: 200
                properties:
                  msg:
                    type: string
                    example: 'Token refreshed successfully.'
                  data:
                    type: object
                    properties:
                      access_token:
                        type: string
                        example: eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...
                      refresh_token:
                        type: string
                        example: eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...
                      expires_in:
                        type: integer
                        example: 3600
                      expiry_date:
                        type: string
                        example: '2026-08-11 15:30:00'
                  success:
                    type: boolean
                    example: true
                  code:
                    type: integer
                    example: 200
        401:
          description: 'Invalid or consumed refresh token'
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                example:
                  msg: 'Invalid refresh token'
                  code: 401
                  success: false
                  error_debug: null
                  error_trace: null
                properties:
                  msg:
                    type: string
                    example: 'Invalid refresh token'
                  code:
                    type: integer
                    example: 401
                  success:
                    type: boolean
                    example: false
                  error_debug:
                    type: string
                    example: null
                  error_trace:
                    type: string
                    example: null
        422:
          description: 'Missing refresh token'
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                example:
                  message: 'The refresh token is required.'
                  errors:
                    refresh_token:
                      - 'The refresh token is required.'
                properties:
                  message:
                    type: string
                    example: 'The refresh token is required.'
                  errors:
                    type: object
                    properties:
                      refresh_token:
                        type: array
                        example:
                          - 'The refresh token is required.'
                        items:
                          type: string
      tags:
        - Authentication
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                refresh_token:
                  type: string
                  description: 'The refresh token returned by login or the previous refresh request.'
                  example: eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9...
                  nullable: false
              required:
                - refresh_token
      security: []

  /api/v1/cards/application:
    get:
      summary: 'Get current card application.'
      operationId: getCurrentCardApplication
      description: "Returns the authenticated user's latest application unless its issued card has been canceled. Canceled-card applications remain stored but are omitted from this frontend state."
      parameters: []
      responses: {  }
      tags:
        - Cards
  /api/v1/cards/applications:
    post:
      summary: 'Start card application.'
      operationId: startCardApplication
      description: "Creates or updates the authenticated user's current card application. If ToS has already been accepted, customer sync is queued immediately."
      parameters: []
      responses: {  }
      tags:
        - Cards
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                card_type:
                  type: string
                  description: 'Card type requested by the user. Must be virtual or physical.'
                  example: virtual
                  nullable: false
                redirect_uri:
                  type: string
                  description: 'Optional redirect URL the card provider should send the user back to after ToS acceptance.'
                  example: 'https://app.orki.io/cards/tos/callback'
                  nullable: true
                kyc_redirect_uri:
                  type: string
                  description: 'Optional redirect URL the hosted KYC provider should send the user back to after KYC completion.'
                  example: 'https://app.orki.io/cards/kyc/callback'
                  nullable: true
              required:
                - card_type
  /api/v1/cards/application/shipping:
    post:
      summary: 'Update physical card shipping details.'
      operationId: updatePhysicalCardShippingDetails
      description: 'Replaces shipping details on a mutable physical card application before the card is issued.'
      parameters: []
      responses: {  }
      tags:
        - Cards
      requestBody:
        required: false
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                application_id:
                  type: string
                  description: "Optional card application ID. Defaults to the authenticated owner's latest physical card application. The <code>id</code> of an existing record in the card_applications table."
                  example: 01KHV4D9TSQ3RY4Y7D8W4Y7SXB
                  nullable: true

                        reference_id: tf_deposit_001

                        description: 'Bank Deposit'

                        amount: '1407.50'

                        occurred_at: '2026-05-24T08:45:00+00:00'
                        memo: 'REF: INV-2026-007'
                        score: 100
                        match_reasons:
                          - invoice_reference_match
                          - amount_exact_match
                          - currency_match
                          - payment_method_match
                          - completed_transaction
                          - time_proximity_match
                        best_match: true
                  success: true
                  code: 200
                properties:
                  msg:
                    type: string
                    example: 'Invoice transaction candidates'
                  data:
                    type: object
                    properties:
                      candidates:
                        type: array
                        example:
                          -
                            transaction_id: TXN-TRF-B98F1E7380
                            reference_id: tf_deposit_001
                            type: deposit
                            direction: incoming
                            method: 'Bank Transfer'
                            description: 'Bank Deposit'
                            asset: USDC
                            amount: '1407.50'
                            status: completed
                            occurred_at: '2026-05-24T08:45:00+00:00'
                            memo: 'REF: INV-2026-007'
                            score: 100
                            match_reasons:
                              - invoice_reference_match
                              - amount_exact_match
                              - currency_match
                              - payment_method_match
                              - completed_transaction
                              - time_proximity_match
                            best_match: true
                        items:
                          type: object
                          properties:
                            transaction_id:
                              type: string
                              example: TXN-TRF-B98F1E7380
                            reference_id:
                              type: string
                              example: tf_deposit_001
                            type:
                              type: string
                              example: deposit
                            direction:
                              type: string
                              example: incoming
                            method:
                              type: string
                              example: 'Bank Transfer'

                              type: string
                              example: 'Bank Deposit'

                              example: USDC

                              type: string
                              example: '1407.50'
                            status:
                              type: string
                              example: completed
                            occurred_at:
                              type: string
                              example: '2026-05-24T08:45:00+00:00'
                            memo:
                              type: string
                              example: 'REF: INV-2026-007'
                            score:
                              type: integer
                              example: 100
                            match_reasons:
                              type: array
                              example:
                                - invoice_reference_match
                                - amount_exact_match
                                - currency_match
                                - payment_method_match
                                - completed_transaction
                                - time_proximity_match

                    example: true

                type: object
                example:

                  msg: 'OTP verified successfully'

                    type: boolean
                    example: true

                    type: string
                    example: 'OTP verified successfully'

                    type: object

                        example: a1b2c3d4e5f6...64chars

                    type: integer
                    example: 200

                type: object

                    example: false
